# Skills · U · part 35

813 entries, unibind-database to unikit-gd-apply. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **unibind-database**: - Queries the UniBind database for experimentally validated transcription factor (TF) binding sites. Use when retrieving direct TF-DNA interaction datasets, downloading binding site coordinates (BED/FASTA) for local analysis, or listing available datasets by species, cell line, or TF name. Don't use to query specific intervals, locations, genes, motif models or expression data. ([aniketpal15/Antigravity-skills](https://github.com/aniketpal15/Antigravity-skills/tree/HEAD/08-databases-and-data-engineering/unibind-database))
- **unibind-search**: Query UniBind for experimentally validated transcription-factor binding sites: retrieve TF-DNA datasets, download binding-site coordinates (BED/FASTA), and list datasets by species, cell line, or TF. Public API, no credential needed. Use for assay-specific binding evidence, not universal occupancy. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/unibind-search) · ★4)

- **unica-build-from-scratch**: Complete end-to-end procedure to build a Samsung custom ROM with UN1CA from a bare machine — install host deps, clone the repo, source the env, download and extract stock firmware, run make_rom, and flash the resulting ZIP. Includes both tracks: building for an already-supported device, and porting UN1CA to a brand-new device from zero. Use this as the master runbook whenever the goal is "build/flash a working ROM" or "bring up a new device," and branch into the specialized skills for depth. ([jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom](https://github.com/jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/unica-build-from-scratch) · 2 copies)
- **unica-build-pipeline**: How to build a ROM with UN1CA end to end — sourcing buildenv.sh, the unica shell-function command system, and the make_rom pipeline (download → extract → hybrid work dir → layered patch → APK rebuild → flashable zip). Use when running builds, invoking unica commands, understanding build caching, forcing rebuilds, or reading out/logs to find which phase failed. The verbatim source of every pipeline script is bundled under references/. ([jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom](https://github.com/jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/unica-build-pipeline) · 2 copies)
- **unica-conveyor**: Конвейер сессий над Unica — координатор и исполнители. - **unica-debloating**: Understand and safely customize UN1CA's debloating system — the single unica/patches/debloat module, its two phases (removing stale ART optimizations, then multi-layer app removal), the global unica/debloat.sh vs target/&lt;device&gt;/debloat.sh lists, the per-partition *_DEBLOAT variables, conditional removals, and the three safe ways to keep or remove an app. Use when a wanted app is missing, when adding/removing bloatware, or when explaining why the ROM strips certain packages. ([jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom](https://github.com/jawadaboumehsen/LtiRom/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/unica-debloating) · 2 copies)

- **unica-flashable-zip**: How UN1CA packages the final flashable ZIP — rebuilding partition images with correct fs_config/file_context metadata, dynamic (super) partitions via lpmake, the dynamic_partitions_op_list, the generated Edify updater-script, OTA metadata, and signing with signapk. Covers the filesystem tools (mkfs.erofs, e2fsdroid/ext4, make_f2fs), img2sdat sparse conversion, and diagnosing flash-time failures like E3051 super-space errors.
